    if k["lock_q"][lock]:
        j = k["lock_q"][lock].pop(0)
        k["lock"][lock] = j
        k["status"][j] = "ready"
    else:
        k["lock"][lock] = None


def wake(k, cv):
    j, lock = k["cv_q"][cv].pop(0)
    if k["lock"][lock] is None:
        k["lock"][lock] = j
        k["status"][j] = "ready"
    else:
        k["lock_q"][lock].append(j)


def step(k, i):
    try:
        request = next(k["threads"][i])
    except StopIteration:
        k["status"][i] = "done"
        return
    if request is None:
        return
    op = request[0]
    if op == "acquire":
        lock = request[1]
        if k["lock"][lock] is None:
            k["lock"][lock] = i
        else:
            k["status"][i] = "blocked"
            k["lock_q"][lock].append(i)
    elif op == "release":
        if k["lock"][request[1]] != i:
            raise RuntimeError(f"thread {i} released a lock it does not hold")
        release(k, request[1])
    elif op == "wait":
        cv, lock = request[1], request[2]
        if k["lock"][lock] != i:
            raise RuntimeError(f"thread {i} waited without holding the lock")
        release(k, lock)
        k["status"][i] = "blocked"
        k["cv_q"][cv].append((i, lock))
    elif op == "signal":
        if k["cv_q"][request[1]]:
            wake(k, request[1])
    elif op == "broadcast":
        while k["cv_q"][request[1]]:
            wake(k, request[1])


def where(k):
    spots = []
    for g in k["threads"]:
        frames = []
        while g is not None and getattr(g, "gi_frame", None) is not None:
            f = g.gi_frame
            frames.append((f.f_lasti, repr(sorted(f.f_locals.items()))))
            g = g.gi_yieldfrom
        spots.append(frames)
    return repr((k["mem"], k["lock"], k["lock_q"], k["cv_q"], k["status"], spots))


def explore(setup, max_steps, bad=None, final=None):
    seen = set()

    def search(prefix):
        k = setup()
        for i in prefix:
            step(k, i)
        key = where(k)
        if key in seen:
            return None
        seen.add(key)
        if bad is not None and bad(k["mem"]):
            return ("bad", prefix)
        ready = [i for i, s in enumerate(k["status"]) if s == "ready"]
        if not ready:
            if "blocked" in k["status"]:
                return ("deadlock", prefix)
            if final is not None and not final(k["mem"]):
                return ("wrong end", prefix)
            return None
        if len(prefix) == max_steps:
            return None
        for i in ready:
            found = search(prefix + [i])
            if found is not None:
                return found
        return None

    return search([])


def worker(mem, me):
    mem["log"].append(("work", me))
    yield from count_down(mem)


def waiter(mem, me):
    yield from wait_zero(mem)
    mem["log"].append(("go", me))


def latch_setup(n, waiters):
    def setup():
        mem = {"count": n, "log": []}
        threads = [worker(mem, i) for i in range(n)] + [waiter(mem, i) for i in range(waiters)]
        return make_monitor_kernel(mem, ["m"], ["zero"], threads)
    return setup


def all_work_before_go(mem):
    work = sum(1 for e in mem["log"] if e[0] == "work")
    return all(sum(1 for e in mem["log"][:i] if e[0] == "work") == work
               for i, e in enumerate(mem["log"]) if e[0] == "go")


def count_down(mem):
    yield ("acquire", "m")
    mem["count"] -= 1
    if mem["count"] == 0:
        yield ("broadcast", "zero")
    yield ("release", "m")


def wait_zero(mem):
    yield ("acquire", "m")
    while mem["count"] > 0:
        yield ("wait", "zero", "m")
    yield ("release", "m")
--- hint
`wait_zero` is the waiting half of a monitor: acquire, `while` the count is above 0 wait on `"zero"`, release. `count_down` is the changing half: acquire, change the count, wake, release.
--- hint
Only the thread that brings the count to 0 needs to wake anyone, and it must wake all the waiters at once with `broadcast`: after the count reaches 0 nobody will ever count down again, so a waiter left asleep would sleep for ever.
--- check test | One thread alone: count down, then wait returns at once
(lambda k: ([step(k, 0) for _ in range(4)], [step(k, 1) for _ in range(3)], k["mem"], k["status"])[2:])(latch_setup(1, 1)()) == ({"count": 0, "log": [("work", 0), ("go", 0)]}, ["done", "done"])
--- check test | A waiter that arrives first sleeps on "zero"
(lambda k: ([step(k, 2) for _ in range(2)], k["status"][2], k["cv_q"]["zero"], k["lock"])[1:])(latch_setup(2, 1)()) == ("blocked", [(2, "m")], {"m": None})
--- check test | Every schedule: two workers, two waiters
explore(latch_setup(2, 2), 300, None, all_work_before_go) is None
--- check test | Every schedule: three workers, two waiters
explore(latch_setup(3, 2), 300, None, all_work_before_go) is None
?? A deadlock means a waiter never woke up: the last count_down must wake every waiter.
--- check test | Every schedule: two workers, three waiters
explore(latch_setup(2, 3), 300, None, all_work_before_go) is None
--- check test | No waiters at all
explore(latch_setup(3, 0), 300, None, lambda m: m["count"] == 0) is None

+++ practice | A counting semaphore from a mutex and a condition variable
--- task
C++20 has `std::counting_semaphore`; before that, programmers built one from a mutex and a condition variable. Build it in the model. The count is `mem["count"]`, the mutex `"m"`, the condition variable `"positive"`.

- `sem_acquire(mem)`: wait until the count is above 0, then take 1 from it (the P of lesson 7).
- `sem_release(mem)`: add 1 to the count and wake one waiting thread (the V).

The starter gives the kernel and explorer, and `sem_setup(count, workers)`: each worker acquires, adds 1 to `mem["inside"]`, pauses for one step, takes 1 from `mem["inside"]`, and releases. The checks explore every schedule, looking for a moment with too many workers inside, and for deadlocks.
--- starter
def make_monitor_kernel(mem, locks, cvs, threads):
    return {
        "mem": mem,
        "lock": {name: None for name in locks},
        "lock_q": {name: [] for name in locks},
        "cv_q": {name: [] for name in cvs},
        "threads": list(threads),
        "status": ["ready"] * len(threads),
    }


def release(k, lock):
    if k["lock_q"][lock]:
        j = k["lock_q"][lock].pop(0)
        k["lock"][lock] = j
        k["status"][j] = "ready"
    else:
        k["lock"][lock] = None


def wake(k, cv):
    j, lock = k["cv_q"][cv].pop(0)
    if k["lock"][lock] is None:
        k["lock"][lock] = j
        k["status"][j] = "ready"
    else:
        k["lock_q"][lock].append(j)


def step(k, i):
    try:
        request = next(k["threads"][i])
    except StopIteration:
        k["status"][i] = "done"
        return
    if request is None:
        return
    op = request[0]
    if op == "acquire":
        lock = request[1]
        if k["lock"][lock] is None:
            k["lock"][lock] = i
        else:
            k["status"][i] = "blocked"
            k["lock_q"][lock].append(i)
    elif op == "release":
        if k["lock"][request[1]] != i:
            raise RuntimeError(f"thread {i} released a lock it does not hold")
        release(k, request[1])
    elif op == "wait":
        cv, lock = request[1], request[2]
        if k["lock"][lock] != i:
            raise RuntimeError(f"thread {i} waited without holding the lock")
        release(k, lock)
        k["status"][i] = "blocked"
        k["cv_q"][cv].append((i, lock))
    elif op == "signal":
        if k["cv_q"][request[1]]:
            wake(k, request[1])
    elif op == "broadcast":
        while k["cv_q"][request[1]]:
            wake(k, request[1])


def where(k):
    spots = []
    for g in k["threads"]:
        frames = []
        while g is not None and getattr(g, "gi_frame", None) is not None:
            f = g.gi_frame
            frames.append((f.f_lasti, repr(sorted(f.f_locals.items()))))
            g = g.gi_yieldfrom
        spots.append(frames)
    return repr((k["mem"], k["lock"], k["lock_q"], k["cv_q"], k["status"], spots))


def explore(setup, max_steps, bad=None, final=None):
    seen = set()

    def search(prefix):
        k = setup()
        for i in prefix:
            step(k, i)
        key = where(k)
        if key in seen:
            return None
        seen.add(key)
        if bad is not None and bad(k["mem"]):
            return ("bad", prefix)
        ready = [i for i, s in enumerate(k["status"]) if s == "ready"]
        if not ready:
            if "blocked" in k["status"]:
                return ("deadlock", prefix)
            if final is not None and not final(k["mem"]):
                return ("wrong end", prefix)
            return None
        if len(prefix) == max_steps:
            return None
        for i in ready:
            found = search(prefix + [i])
            if found is not None:
                return found
        return None

    return search([])


def worker(mem):
    yield from sem_acquire(mem)
    mem["inside"] += 1
    yield
    mem["inside"] -= 1
    yield from sem_release(mem)


def sem_setup(count, workers):
    def setup():
        mem = {"count": count, "inside": 0}
        return make_monitor_kernel(mem, ["m"], ["positive"], [worker(mem) for _ in range(workers)])
    return setup


def sem_acquire(mem):
    yield ("acquire", "m")
    mem["count"] -= 1
    yield ("release", "m")


def sem_release(mem):
    yield ("acquire", "m")
    mem["count"] += 1
    yield ("release", "m")
--- solution
def make_monitor_kernel(mem, locks, cvs, threads):
    return {
        "mem": mem,
        "lock": {name: None for name in locks},
        "lock_q": {name: [] for name in locks},
        "cv_q": {name: [] for name in cvs},
        "threads": list(threads),
        "status": ["ready"] * len(threads),
    }


def release(k, lock):
    if k["lock_q"][lock]:
        j = k["lock_q"][lock].pop(0)
        k["lock"][lock] = j
        k["status"][j] = "ready"
    else:
        k["lock"][lock] = None


def wake(k, cv):
    j, lock = k["cv_q"][cv].pop(0)
    if k["lock"][lock] is None:
        k["lock"][lock] = j
        k["status"][j] = "ready"
    else:
        k["lock_q"][lock].append(j)


def step(k, i):
    try:
        request = next(k["threads"][i])
    except StopIteration:
        k["status"][i] = "done"
        return
    if request is None:
        return
    op = request[0]
    if op == "acquire":
        lock = request[1]
        if k["lock"][lock] is None:
            k["lock"][lock] = i
        else:
            k["status"][i] = "blocked"
            k["lock_q"][lock].append(i)
    elif op == "release":
        if k["lock"][request[1]] != i:
            raise RuntimeError(f"thread {i} released a lock it does not hold")
        release(k, request[1])
    elif op == "wait":
        cv, lock = request[1], request[2]
        if k["lock"][lock] != i:
            raise RuntimeError(f"thread {i} waited without holding the lock")
        release(k, lock)
        k["status"][i] = "blocked"
        k["cv_q"][cv].append((i, lock))
    elif op == "signal":
        if k["cv_q"][request[1]]:
            wake(k, request[1])
    elif op == "broadcast":
        while k["cv_q"][request[1]]:
            wake(k, request[1])


def where(k):
    spots = []
    for g in k["threads"]:
        frames = []
        while g is not None and getattr(g, "gi_frame", None) is not None:
            f = g.gi_frame
            frames.append((f.f_lasti, repr(sorted(f.f_locals.items()))))
            g = g.gi_yieldfrom
        spots.append(frames)
    return repr((k["mem"], k["lock"], k["lock_q"], k["cv_q"], k["status"], spots))


def explore(setup, max_steps, bad=None, final=None):
    seen = set()

    def search(prefix):
        k = setup()
        for i in prefix:
            step(k, i)
        key = where(k)
        if key in seen:
            return None
        seen.add(key)
        if bad is not None and bad(k["mem"]):
            return ("bad", prefix)
        ready = [i for i, s in enumerate(k["status"]) if s == "ready"]
        if not ready:
            if "blocked" in k["status"]:
                return ("deadlock", prefix)
            if final is not None and not final(k["mem"]):
                return ("wrong end", prefix)
            return None
        if len(prefix) == max_steps:
            return None
        for i in ready:
            found = search(prefix + [i])
            if found is not None:
                return found
        return None

    return search([])


def worker(mem):
    yield from sem_acquire(mem)
    mem["inside"] += 1
    yield
    mem["inside"] -= 1
    yield from sem_release(mem)


def sem_setup(count, workers):
    def setup():
        mem = {"count": count, "inside": 0}
        return make_monitor_kernel(mem, ["m"], ["positive"], [worker(mem) for _ in range(workers)])
    return setup


def sem_acquire(mem):
    yield ("acquire", "m")
    while mem["count"] == 0:
        yield ("wait", "positive", "m")
    mem["count"] -= 1
    yield ("release", "m")


def sem_release(mem):
    yield ("acquire", "m")
    mem["count"] += 1
    yield ("signal", "positive")
    yield ("release", "m")
--- hint
The count can never go below 0, so `sem_acquire` must wait while it is 0, in a `while` loop, and only then take 1.
--- hint
One release makes room for exactly one more thread, so `signal` is enough in `sem_release`. Signal after adding to the count, while you still hold the mutex.
--- check test | With a count of 1, at most one worker is inside, in every schedule
explore(sem_setup(1, 3), 300, lambda m: m["inside"] > 1, lambda m: m["count"] == 1 and m["inside"] == 0) is None
--- check test | With a count of 2, at most two are inside
explore(sem_setup(2, 3), 300, lambda m: m["inside"] > 2, lambda m: m["count"] == 2) is None
--- check test | ... and two really can be inside at once
(lambda r: r is not None and r[0] == "bad")(explore(sem_setup(2, 3), 300, lambda m: m["inside"] > 1))
--- check test | The count never goes negative
explore(sem_setup(1, 3), 300, lambda m: m["count"] < 0) is None
--- check test | A count of 0 blocks everyone: the explorer reports the deadlock
(lambda r: r is not None and r[0] == "deadlock")(explore(sem_setup(0, 2), 100))

+++ practice | Transfers that cannot deadlock
--- task
Each bank account has its own mutex: account `i`'s lock is named `f"acct{i}"`, and `mem["bal"]` is the list of balances. A transfer must hold both accounts' locks while it moves the money.

Write the generator `transfer(mem, a, b, amount)` so that no schedule can deadlock, however many tellers run transfers in whatever directions at once: acquire the two locks in **increasing account number** order (the global lock order of lesson 9, which `std::scoped_lock` makes unnecessary in C++), take `amount` from account `a`, add it to account `b`, then release both. `a` and `b` are always different.

The starter gives the kernel and explorer, and `bank_setup(balances, tellers)`, where `tellers` is a list of job lists `(a, b, amount)`; each teller runs its jobs in order.
--- starter
def make_monitor_kernel(mem, locks, cvs, threads):
    return {
        "mem": mem,
        "lock": {name: None for name in locks},
        "lock_q": {name: [] for name in locks},
        "cv_q": {name: [] for name in cvs},
        "threads": list(threads),
        "status": ["ready"] * len(threads),
    }


def release(k, lock):
    if k["lock_q"][lock]:
        j = k["lock_q"][lock].pop(0)
        k["lock"][lock] = j
        k["status"][j] = "ready"
    else:
        k["lock"][lock] = None


def wake(k, cv):
    j, lock = k["cv_q"][cv].pop(0)
    if k["lock"][lock] is None:
        k["lock"][lock] = j
        k["status"][j] = "ready"
    else:
        k["lock_q"][lock].append(j)


def step(k, i):
    try:
        request = next(k["threads"][i])
    except StopIteration:
        k["status"][i] = "done"
        return
    if request is None:
        return
    op = request[0]
    if op == "acquire":
        lock = request[1]
        if k["lock"][lock] is None:
            k["lock"][lock] = i
        else:
            k["status"][i] = "blocked"
            k["lock_q"][lock].append(i)
    elif op == "release":
        if k["lock"][request[1]] != i:
            raise RuntimeError(f"thread {i} released a lock it does not hold")
        release(k, request[1])
    elif op == "wait":
        cv, lock = request[1], request[2]
        if k["lock"][lock] != i:
            raise RuntimeError(f"thread {i} waited without holding the lock")
        release(k, lock)
        k["status"][i] = "blocked"
        k["cv_q"][cv].append((i, lock))
    elif op == "signal":
        if k["cv_q"][request[1]]:
            wake(k, request[1])
    elif op == "broadcast":
        while k["cv_q"][request[1]]:
            wake(k, request[1])


def where(k):
    spots = []
    for g in k["threads"]:
        frames = []
        while g is not None and getattr(g, "gi_frame", None) is not None:
            f = g.gi_frame
            frames.append((f.f_lasti, repr(sorted(f.f_locals.items()))))
            g = g.gi_yieldfrom
        spots.append(frames)
    return repr((k["mem"], k["lock"], k["lock_q"], k["cv_q"], k["status"], spots))


def explore(setup, max_steps, bad=None, final=None):
    seen = set()

    def search(prefix):
        k = setup()
        for i in prefix:
            step(k, i)
        key = where(k)
        if key in seen:
            return None
        seen.add(key)
        if bad is not None and bad(k["mem"]):
            return ("bad", prefix)
        ready = [i for i, s in enumerate(k["status"]) if s == "ready"]
        if not ready:
            if "blocked" in k["status"]:
                return ("deadlock", prefix)
            if final is not None and not final(k["mem"]):
                return ("wrong end", prefix)
            return None
        if len(prefix) == max_steps:
            return None
        for i in ready:
            found = search(prefix + [i])
            if found is not None:
                return found
        return None

    return search([])


def teller(mem, jobs):
    for a, b, amount in jobs:
        yield from transfer(mem, a, b, amount)


def bank_setup(balances, tellers):
    def setup():
        mem = {"bal": list(balances)}
        locks = [f"acct{i}" for i in range(len(balances))]
        return make_monitor_kernel(mem, locks, [], [teller(mem, jobs) for jobs in tellers])
    return setup


def transfer(mem, a, b, amount):
    yield ("acquire", f"acct{a}")
    yield ("acquire", f"acct{b}")
    mem["bal"][a] -= amount
    mem["bal"][b] += amount
    yield ("release", f"acct{b}")
    yield ("release", f"acct{a}")
--- solution
def make_monitor_kernel(mem, locks, cvs, threads):
    return {
        "mem": mem,
        "lock": {name: None for name in locks},
        "lock_q": {name: [] for name in locks},
        "cv_q": {name: [] for name in cvs},
        "threads": list(threads),
        "status": ["ready"] * len(threads),
    }

    if k["lock_q"][lock]:
        j = k["lock_q"][lock].pop(0)
        k["lock"][lock] = j
        k["status"][j] = "ready"
    else:
        k["lock"][lock] = None


def wake(k, cv):
    j, lock = k["cv_q"][cv].pop(0)
    if k["lock"][lock] is None:
        k["lock"][lock] = j
        k["status"][j] = "ready"
    else:
        k["lock_q"][lock].append(j)


def step(k, i):
    try:
        request = next(k["threads"][i])
    except StopIteration:
        k["status"][i] = "done"
        return
    if request is None:
        return
    op = request[0]
    if op == "acquire":
        lock = request[1]
        if k["lock"][lock] is None:
            k["lock"][lock] = i
        else:
            k["status"][i] = "blocked"
            k["lock_q"][lock].append(i)
    elif op == "release":
        if k["lock"][request[1]] != i:
            raise RuntimeError(f"thread {i} released a lock it does not hold")
        release(k, request[1])
    elif op == "wait":
        cv, lock = request[1], request[2]
        if k["lock"][lock] != i:
            raise RuntimeError(f"thread {i} waited without holding the lock")
        release(k, lock)
        k["status"][i] = "blocked"
        k["cv_q"][cv].append((i, lock))
    elif op == "signal":
        if k["cv_q"][request[1]]:
            wake(k, request[1])
    elif op == "broadcast":
        while k["cv_q"][request[1]]:
            wake(k, request[1])

    spots = []
    for g in k["threads"]:
        frames = []
        while g is not None and getattr(g, "gi_frame", None) is not None:
            f = g.gi_frame
            frames.append((f.f_lasti, repr(sorted(f.f_locals.items()))))
            g = g.gi_yieldfrom
        spots.append(frames)
    return repr((k["mem"], k["lock"], k["lock_q"], k["cv_q"], k["status"], spots))


def explore(setup, max_steps, bad=None, final=None):
    seen = set()

    def search(prefix):
        k = setup()
        for i in prefix:
            step(k, i)
        key = where(k)
        if key in seen:
            return None
        seen.add(key)
        if bad is not None and bad(k["mem"]):
            return ("bad", prefix)
        ready = [i for i, s in enumerate(k["status"]) if s == "ready"]
        if not ready:
            if "blocked" in k["status"]:
                return ("deadlock", prefix)
            if final is not None and not final(k["mem"]):
                return ("wrong end", prefix)
            return None
        if len(prefix) == max_steps:
            return None
        for i in ready:
            found = search(prefix + [i])
            if found is not None:
                return found
        return None

    return search([])


def teller(mem, jobs):
    for a, b, amount in jobs:
        yield from transfer(mem, a, b, amount)


def bank_setup(balances, tellers):
    def setup():
        mem = {"bal": list(balances)}
        locks = [f"acct{i}" for i in range(len(balances))]
        return make_monitor_kernel(mem, locks, [], [teller(mem, jobs) for jobs in tellers])
    return setup


def transfer(mem, a, b, amount):
    first, second = min(a, b), max(a, b)
    yield ("acquire", f"acct{first}")
    yield ("acquire", f"acct{second}")
    mem["bal"][a] -= amount
    mem["bal"][b] += amount
    yield ("release", f"acct{second}")
    yield ("release", f"acct{first}")
--- hint
The starter locks `a` first. Two opposite transfers, 0 to 1 and 1 to 0, can each take their first lock and then wait for the other's: a cycle in the wait-for graph.
--- hint
Lock `min(a, b)` first and `max(a, b)` second, whichever way the money goes. The money still moves from `a` to `b`.
--- check test | Opposite transfers between two accounts
explore(bank_setup([100, 100], [[(0, 1, 10)], [(1, 0, 5)]]), 300, None, lambda m: m["bal"] == [95, 105]) is None
--- check test | A cycle of three transfers
explore(bank_setup([50, 50, 50], [[(0, 1, 1)], [(1, 2, 2)], [(2, 0, 3)]]), 300, None, lambda m: m["bal"] == [52, 49, 49]) is None
--- check test | Tellers with several jobs each; money is never created or lost
explore(bank_setup([100, 100, 100], [[(0, 1, 10), (1, 2, 3)], [(2, 0, 5)], [(1, 0, 1)]]), 400, lambda m: m["bal"] is None, lambda m: sum(m["bal"]) == 300 and m["bal"] == [96, 106, 98]) is None
--- check test | One teller alone still works
(lambda k: ([step(k, 0) for _ in range(12)], k["mem"]["bal"], k["status"])[1:])(bank_setup([10, 0, 0], [[(0, 2, 4), (2, 1, 1)]])()) == ([6, 1, 3], ["done"])

+++ practice | A barrier you can use again and again
--- task
A **barrier** (C++20's `std::barrier`) makes `n` threads wait for each other: nobody leaves round r until all `n` have arrived for round r. It must work for many rounds in a row, and that is where naive versions break: a fast thread that leaves round r and arrives for round r + 1 must not confuse the threads still leaving round r.

Model it. `mem["n"]` is the number of threads, `mem["arrived"]` counts arrivals in the current round, and `mem["gen"]` is the round number (the **generation**), all starting at their values in `mem`. The mutex is `"m"` and the condition variable `"turn"`. Write the generator `arrive_and_wait(mem)`:

- Add 1 to `arrived`. If that makes it `n`, this is the last thread of the round: set `arrived` back to 0, add 1 to `gen`, and wake every waiting thread.
- Otherwise, remember the generation you arrived in, and wait until `gen` has changed.

It takes the mutex first and releases it before returning. With `n` equal to 1 it never waits. The starter gives the kernel and explorer, `barrier_setup(n, rounds)` (each thread logs `("arrive", i, r)` before and `("pass", i, r)` after each call), and `rounds_ok(mem)`, which checks that in every round all arrivals come before any pass.
--- starter
def make_monitor_kernel(mem, locks, cvs, threads):
    return {
        "mem": mem,
        "lock": {name: None for name in locks},
        "lock_q": {name: [] for name in locks},
        "cv_q": {name: [] for name in cvs},
        "threads": list(threads),
        "status": ["ready"] * len(threads),
    }


def release(k, lock):
    if k["lock_q"][lock]:
        j = k["lock_q"][lock].pop(0)
        k["lock"][lock] = j
        k["status"][j] = "ready"
    else:
        k["lock"][lock] = None


def wake(k, cv):
    j, lock = k["cv_q"][cv].pop(0)
    if k["lock"][lock] is None:
        k["lock"][lock] = j
        k["status"][j] = "ready"
    else:
        k["lock_q"][lock].append(j)


def step(k, i):
    try:
        request = next(k["threads"][i])
    except StopIteration:
        k["status"][i] = "done"
        return
    if request is None:
        return
    op = request[0]
    if op == "acquire":
        lock = request[1]
        if k["lock"][lock] is None:
            k["lock"][lock] = i
        else:
            k["status"][i] = "blocked"
            k["lock_q"][lock].append(i)
    elif op == "release":
        if k["lock"][request[1]] != i:
            raise RuntimeError(f"thread {i} released a lock it does not hold")
        release(k, request[1])
    elif op == "wait":
        cv, lock = request[1], request[2]
        if k["lock"][lock] != i:
            raise RuntimeError(f"thread {i} waited without holding the lock")
        release(k, lock)
        k["status"][i] = "blocked"
        k["cv_q"][cv].append((i, lock))
    elif op == "signal":
        if k["cv_q"][request[1]]:
            wake(k, request[1])
    elif op == "broadcast":
        while k["cv_q"][request[1]]:
            wake(k, request[1])


def where(k):
    spots = []
    for g in k["threads"]:
        frames = []
        while g is not None and getattr(g, "gi_frame", None) is not None:
            f = g.gi_frame
            frames.append((f.f_lasti, repr(sorted(f.f_locals.items()))))
            g = g.gi_yieldfrom
        spots.append(frames)
    return repr((k["mem"], k["lock"], k["lock_q"], k["cv_q"], k["status"], spots))


def explore(setup, max_steps, bad=None, final=None):
    seen = set()

    def search(prefix):
        k = setup()
        for i in prefix:
            step(k, i)
        key = where(k)
        if key in seen:
            return None
        seen.add(key)
        if bad is not None and bad(k["mem"]):
            return ("bad", prefix)
        ready = [i for i, s in enumerate(k["status"]) if s == "ready"]
        if not ready:
            if "blocked" in k["status"]:
                return ("deadlock", prefix)
            if final is not None and not final(k["mem"]):
                return ("wrong end", prefix)
            return None
        if len(prefix) == max_steps:
            return None
        for i in ready:
            found = search(prefix + [i])
            if found is not None:
                return found
        return None

    return search([])


def worker(mem, me, rounds):
    for r in range(rounds):
        mem["log"].append(("arrive", me, r))
        yield from arrive_and_wait(mem)
        mem["log"].append(("pass", me, r))


def barrier_setup(n, rounds):
    def setup():
        mem = {"n": n, "arrived": 0, "gen": 0, "log": []}
        return make_monitor_kernel(mem, ["m"], ["turn"], [worker(mem, i, rounds) for i in range(n)])
    return setup


def rounds_ok(mem):
    log = mem["log"]
    for r in {e[2] for e in log}:
        arrivals = [i for i, e in enumerate(log) if e[0] == "arrive" and e[2] == r]
        passes = [i for i, e in enumerate(log) if e[0] == "pass" and e[2] == r]
        if len(arrivals) != mem["n"] or len(passes) != mem["n"] or max(arrivals) > min(passes):
            return False
    return True


def arrive_and_wait(mem):
    yield ("acquire", "m")
    mem["arrived"] += 1
    if mem["arrived"] == mem["n"]:
        yield ("broadcast", "turn")
    else:
        while mem["arrived"] < mem["n"]:
            yield ("wait", "turn", "m")
    yield ("release", "m")
--- solution
def make_monitor_kernel(mem, locks, cvs, threads):
    return {
        "mem": mem,
        "lock": {name: None for name in locks},
        "lock_q": {name: [] for name in locks},
        "cv_q": {name: [] for name in cvs},
        "threads": list(threads),
        "status": ["ready"] * len(threads),
    }


def release(k, lock):
    if k["lock_q"][lock]:
        j = k["lock_q"][lock].pop(0)
        k["lock"][lock] = j
        k["status"][j] = "ready"
    else:
        k["lock"][lock] = None


def wake(k, cv):
    j, lock = k["cv_q"][cv].pop(0)
    if k["lock"][lock] is None:
        k["lock"][lock] = j
        k["status"][j] = "ready"
    else:
        k["lock_q"][lock].append(j)


def step(k, i):
    try:
        request = next(k["threads"][i])
    except StopIteration:
        k["status"][i] = "done"
        return
    if request is None:
        return
    op = request[0]
    if op == "acquire":
        lock = request[1]
        if k["lock"][lock] is None:
            k["lock"][lock] = i
        else:
            k["status"][i] = "blocked"
            k["lock_q"][lock].append(i)
    elif op == "release":
        if k["lock"][request[1]] != i:
            raise RuntimeError(f"thread {i} released a lock it does not hold")
        release(k, request[1])
    elif op == "wait":
        cv, lock = request[1], request[2]
        if k["lock"][lock] != i:
            raise RuntimeError(f"thread {i} waited without holding the lock")
        release(k, lock)
        k["status"][i] = "blocked"
        k["cv_q"][cv].append((i, lock))
    elif op == "signal":
        if k["cv_q"][request[1]]:
            wake(k, request[1])
    elif op == "broadcast":
        while k["cv_q"][request[1]]:
            wake(k, request[1])


def where(k):
    spots = []
    for g in k["threads"]:
        frames = []
        while g is not None and getattr(g, "gi_frame", None) is not None:
            f = g.gi_frame
            frames.append((f.f_lasti, repr(sorted(f.f_locals.items()))))
            g = g.gi_yieldfrom
        spots.append(frames)
    return repr((k["mem"], k["lock"], k["lock_q"], k["cv_q"], k["status"], spots))


def explore(setup, max_steps, bad=None, final=None):
    seen = set()

    def search(prefix):
        k = setup()
        for i in prefix:
            step(k, i)
        key = where(k)
        if key in seen:
            return None
        seen.add(key)
        if bad is not None and bad(k["mem"]):
            return ("bad", prefix)
        ready = [i for i, s in enumerate(k["status"]) if s == "ready"]
        if not ready:
            if "blocked" in k["status"]:
                return ("deadlock", prefix)
            if final is not None and not final(k["mem"]):
                return ("wrong end", prefix)
            return None
        if len(prefix) == max_steps:
            return None
        for i in ready:
            found = search(prefix + [i])
            if found is not None:
                return found
        return None

    return search([])


def worker(mem, me, rounds):
    for r in range(rounds):
        mem["log"].append(("arrive", me, r))
        yield from arrive_and_wait(mem)
        mem["log"].append(("pass", me, r))


def barrier_setup(n, rounds):
    def setup():
        mem = {"n": n, "arrived": 0, "gen": 0, "log": []}
        return make_monitor_kernel(mem, ["m"], ["turn"], [worker(mem, i, rounds) for i in range(n)])
    return setup


def rounds_ok(mem):
    log = mem["log"]
    for r in {e[2] for e in log}:
        arrivals = [i for i, e in enumerate(log) if e[0] == "arrive" and e[2] == r]
        passes = [i for i, e in enumerate(log) if e[0] == "pass" and e[2] == r]
        if len(arrivals) != mem["n"] or len(passes) != mem["n"] or max(arrivals) > min(passes):
            return False
    return True


def arrive_and_wait(mem):
    yield ("acquire", "m")
    my_gen = mem["gen"]
    mem["arrived"] += 1
    if mem["arrived"] == mem["n"]:
        mem["arrived"] = 0
        mem["gen"] += 1
        yield ("broadcast", "turn")
    else:
        while mem["gen"] == my_gen:
            yield ("wait", "turn", "m")
    yield ("release", "m")
--- hint
The starter never resets `arrived`, so the second round sees the count already at `n` and nobody waits. But resetting it to 0 is not enough on its own: waiters then cannot tell "the round is over" from "nobody has arrived yet".
--- hint
Wait on the generation instead of the count: read `mem["gen"]` into a local when you arrive, and loop `while mem["gen"] == my_gen`. The last thread resets the count, bumps the generation and broadcasts.
--- check test | Every schedule: two threads, three rounds
explore(barrier_setup(2, 3), 400, None, rounds_ok) is None
--- check test | Every schedule: three threads, two rounds
explore(barrier_setup(3, 2), 400, None, rounds_ok) is None
?? "wrong end" means some thread passed a round before everyone had arrived, usually in the second round: the count must start again from 0.
--- check test | One thread never waits
(lambda k: ([step(k, 0) for _ in range(12)], k["status"], k["mem"]["gen"], k["mem"]["arrived"])[1:])(barrier_setup(1, 3)()) == (["done"], 3, 0)
--- check test | After a round the count is back at 0 and the generation has moved on
explore(barrier_setup(3, 1), 300, None, lambda m: m["arrived"] == 0 and m["gen"] == 1 and rounds_ok(m)) is None
--- check test | The first thread to arrive sleeps
(lambda k: ([step(k, 1) for _ in range(2)], k["status"], k["mem"]["arrived"])[1:])(barrier_setup(2, 1)()) == (["ready", "blocked"], 1)

+++ practice | Debug: the dispatcher worker that crashes
--- task
A ground station hands decoding jobs to workers through a list `mem["jobs"]`, guarded by the mutex `"m"`, with the condition variable `"has_job"`. `take_job(mem)` should return the oldest job, waiting while there is none. `add_job(mem, job)` appends a job and signals. Each worker takes exactly the number of jobs it was told to.

It has a bug. Most runs work, but with two workers now and then one of them crashes with `IndexError: pop from empty list`. The explorer finds the schedule every time. Find the bug and fix it.
--- starter
def make_monitor_kernel(mem, locks, cvs, threads):
    return {
        "mem": mem,
        "lock": {name: None for name in locks},
        "lock_q": {name: [] for name in locks},
        "cv_q": {name: [] for name in cvs},
        "threads": list(threads),
        "status": ["ready"] * len(threads),
    }


def release(k, lock):
    if k["lock_q"][lock]:
        j = k["lock_q"][lock].pop(0)
        k["lock"][lock] = j
        k["status"][j] = "ready"
    else:
        k["lock"][lock] = None


def wake(k, cv):
    j, lock = k["cv_q"][cv].pop(0)
    if k["lock"][lock] is None:
        k["lock"][lock] = j
        k["status"][j] = "ready"
    else:
        k["lock_q"][lock].append(j)


def step(k, i):
    try:
        request = next(k["threads"][i])
    except StopIteration:
        k["status"][i] = "done"
        return
    if request is None:
        return
    op = request[0]
    if op == "acquire":
        lock = request[1]
        if k["lock"][lock] is None:
            k["lock"][lock] = i
        else:
            k["status"][i] = "blocked"
            k["lock_q"][lock].append(i)
    elif op == "release":
        if k["lock"][request[1]] != i:
            raise RuntimeError(f"thread {i} released a lock it does not hold")
        release(k, request[1])
    elif op == "wait":
        cv, lock = request[1], request[2]
        if k["lock"][lock] != i:
            raise RuntimeError(f"thread {i} waited without holding the lock")
        release(k, lock)
        k["status"][i] = "blocked"
        k["cv_q"][cv].append((i, lock))
    elif op == "signal":
        if k["cv_q"][request[1]]:
            wake(k, request[1])
    elif op == "broadcast":
        while k["cv_q"][request[1]]:
            wake(k, request[1])


def where(k):
    spots = []
    for g in k["threads"]:
        frames = []
        while g is not None and getattr(g, "gi_frame", None) is not None:
            f = g.gi_frame
            frames.append((f.f_lasti, repr(sorted(f.f_locals.items()))))
            g = g.gi_yieldfrom
        spots.append(frames)
    return repr((k["mem"], k["lock"], k["lock_q"], k["cv_q"], k["status"], spots))


def explore(setup, max_steps, bad=None, final=None):
    seen = set()

    def search(prefix):
        k = setup()
        for i in prefix:
            step(k, i)
        key = where(k)
        if key in seen:
            return None
        seen.add(key)
        if bad is not None and bad(k["mem"]):
            return ("bad", prefix)
        ready = [i for i, s in enumerate(k["status"]) if s == "ready"]
        if not ready:
            if "blocked" in k["status"]:
                return ("deadlock", prefix)
            if final is not None and not final(k["mem"]):
                return ("wrong end", prefix)
            return None
        if len(prefix) == max_steps:
            return None
        for i in ready:
            found = search(prefix + [i])
            if found is not None:
                return found
        return None

    return search([])


def add_job(mem, job):
    yield ("acquire", "m")
    mem["jobs"].append(job)
    yield ("signal", "has_job")
    yield ("release", "m")


def take_job(mem):
    yield ("acquire", "m")
    if not mem["jobs"]:
        yield ("wait", "has_job", "m")
    job = mem["jobs"].pop(0)
    yield ("release", "m")
    return job


def dispatcher(mem, jobs):
    for job in jobs:
        yield from add_job(mem, job)


def worker(mem, count):
    for _ in range(count):
        job = yield from take_job(mem)
        mem["done"].append(job)


def jobs_setup(jobs, counts):
    def setup():
        mem = {"jobs": [], "done": []}
        threads = [dispatcher(mem, jobs)] + [worker(mem, c) for c in counts]
        return make_monitor_kernel(mem, ["m"], ["has_job"], threads)
    return setup
--- solution
def make_monitor_kernel(mem, locks, cvs, threads):
    return {
        "mem": mem,
        "lock": {name: None for name in locks},
        "lock_q": {name: [] for name in locks},
        "cv_q": {name: [] for name in cvs},
        "threads": list(threads),
        "status": ["ready"] * len(threads),
    }


def release(k, lock):
    if k["lock_q"][lock]:
        j = k["lock_q"][lock].pop(0)
        k["lock"][lock] = j
        k["status"][j] = "ready"
    else:
        k["lock"][lock] = None


def wake(k, cv):
    j, lock = k["cv_q"][cv].pop(0)
    if k["lock"][lock] is None:
        k["lock"][lock] = j
        k["status"][j] = "ready"
    else:
        k["lock_q"][lock].append(j)


def step(k, i):
    try:
        request = next(k["threads"][i])
    except StopIteration:
        k["status"][i] = "done"
        return
    if request is None:
        return
    op = request[0]
    if op == "acquire":
        lock = request[1]
        if k["lock"][lock] is None:
            k["lock"][lock] = i
        else:
            k["status"][i] = "blocked"
            k["lock_q"][lock].append(i)
    elif op == "release":
        if k["lock"][request[1]] != i:
            raise RuntimeError(f"thread {i} released a lock it does not hold")
        release(k, request[1])
    elif op == "wait":
        cv, lock = request[1], request[2]
        if k["lock"][lock] != i:
            raise RuntimeError(f"thread {i} waited without holding the lock")
        release(k, lock)
        k["status"][i] = "blocked"
        k["cv_q"][cv].append((i, lock))
    elif op == "signal":
        if k["cv_q"][request[1]]:
            wake(k, request[1])
    elif op == "broadcast":
        while k["cv_q"][request[1]]:
            wake(k, request[1])

            f = g.gi_frame
            frames.append((f.f_lasti, repr(sorted(f.f_locals.items()))))
            g = g.gi_yieldfrom
        spots.append(frames)
    return repr((k["mem"], k["lock"], k["lock_q"], k["cv_q"], k["status"], spots))


def explore(setup, max_steps, bad=None, final=None):
    seen = set()

    def search(prefix):

            step(k, i)
        key = where(k)
        if key in seen:
            return None
        seen.add(key)
        if bad is not None and bad(k["mem"]):
            return ("bad", prefix)
